It builds the evaluation examples, monthly KPI and model head-to-head\nthat feed the monthly forecast accuracy pack and the desk scorecard.\n\n```\npython -m fcaccuracy build --as-of 2026-09-22T06:00:00Z     # writes out/accuracy/\n```\n\n`--as-of` is the UTC instant the mart is built for (the nightly job passes its start time). Options: `--db`\n(default `data/warehouse.sqlite`), `--out` (default `out/accuracy`).\n\n## Layout\n\n| Path | What |\n|---|---|\n| `fcaccuracy/` | the mart (`cli.py`, `warehouse.py`, `mart.py`, `kpi.py`, `outputs.py`) |\n| `sql/accuracy_examples.sql` | example query used by `mart.py` |\n| `data/warehouse.sqlite` | warehouse extract (settlement, forecasts, portfolios, models, KPI close log) |\n| `docs/mart/accuracy_mart.md` | output specification |\n| `docs/kpi/`, `docs/finance/` | KPI definition, scorecard policy, retired Billing feeds |\n| `docs/settlement/` | how settlement volumes are published |\n| `docs/trading/`, `docs/forecasting/`, `docs/models/` | day-ahead process, forecast store, model notes |\n| `docs/data/data_dictionary.md` | warehouse tables and views |\n| `docs/portfolio/` | portfolio restructures |\n| `notebooks/kpi_pack_legacy.ipynb` | the notebook that produced packs until June 2026 |\n| `reports/` | signed-off packs, the September accuracy review, ops dashboard |\n| `notes/`, `ops/` | team thread, settlement incidents |\n| `RELEASES.md` | mart release notes |\n\nPython 3.12 with pandas; Any error becomes an\nimbalance position. The KPI measures how accurate the forecasts the desk trades on are against the volume the desk\nis charged imbalance on.\n\n## Definition\n\n- **Forecast unit:** supply region x portfolio x delivery day.\n- **Horizon:** days from the forecast run day to the delivery day, for every horizon the day-ahead process issues\n  (currently D+1 to D+7).\n- **Forecast evaluated:** for each run day and horizon, the forecast the desk traded on (see\n  `docs/trading/day_ahead_process.md`).\n- **Actual:** the settled volume on which the imbalance charge for the delivery day is calculated (see\n  `docs/settlement/settlement_process.md`).\n- **Metric:** WAPE = sum of |forecast - actual| / sum of actual, over scored forecasts.\n- **Reporting grain:** model x delivery month x portfolio x horizon, pooled across regions.\n- **Models:** every model run through the day-ahead process is measured, production and shadow, so candidate models\n  can be compared with the production model on the same forecasts.\n\n## Publication\n\nThe KPI for a delivery month is published in the monthly **forecast accuracy pack** at KPI close, and the close is\nrecorded in `kpi_close_log`. The pack is signed off by the Head of Trading Analytics and feeds the desk scorecard\n(`docs/finance/scorecard_policy.md`). Forecasts without a settled volume are shown in the pack's forecast count but not\nscored.\n# Accuracy mart: output specification\n\nOwner: Trading Analytics. Written as the requirement for replacing the pack notebook (June 2026); the mart must keep\nproducing these outputs.\n\n`python -m fcaccuracy build --as-of <UTC instant>` writes three files to `out/accuracy/`. Numbers are written\nunrounded. Only **closed KPI months**\nare included: delivery months whose KPI close is recorded in `kpi_close_log` at or before `--as-of`.\n\n## `evaluation_examples.csv`\n\nOne row per model, region, portfolio, delivery day and horizon for every forecast the KPI evaluates in closed months.\n\n| Column | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `model` | forecasting model |\n| `run_date` | forecast run day |\n| `issue_id` | forecast issue the evaluated value comes from |\n| `region`, `portfolio` | forecast unit |\n| `target_date` | delivery day |\n| `horizon` | days from run day to delivery day |\n| `kpi_month` | KPI month (`YYYY-MM`) the forecast belongs to |\n| `forecast_mwh` | evaluated forecast |\n| `status` | `scored`: a settled volume is available and the forecast is scored; `unsettled`: no settled volume is available, so the forecast is counted but not scored |\n| `actual_run_ids` | settlement run ids whose volumes make up the actual, `;`-separated, ascending (empty when unsettled) |\n| `actual_mwh` | actual volume (empty when unsettled) |\n| `abs_error_mwh` | absolute error in MWh (empty when unsettled) |\n\n## `monthly_kpi.csv`\n\nOne row per model, KPI month, portfolio and horizon with at least one example.\n\n| Column | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `n_examples` | examples |\n| `n_scored` | scored examples |\n| `abs_error_mwh`, `actual_mwh` | sums over scored examples |\n| `wape` | `abs_error_mwh / actual_mwh` (empty when nothing is scored) |\n\n## `summary.json`\n\n```json\n{\"as_of\": \"...\", \"closed_months\": [\"2025-07\", \"...\"],\n \"head_to_head\": [{\"model_a\": \"v3\", \"model_b\": \"v4\", \"n_pairs\": 0, \"first_month\": \"...\", \"last_month\": \"...\",\n                   \"wape_a\": 0.0, \"wape_b\": 0.0, \"relative_change\": 0.0}]}\n```\n\n`head_to_head` has one entry per pair of models (`model_a` < `model_b`) for which a comparison exists, comparing them\non the forecasts both models have scored (same region, portfolio, delivery day and horizon) in the closed months.\n`n_pairs` is the number of such forecasts, `first_month`/`last_month` their KPI month range, `wape_*` the pooled WAPE\nof each model on them, and `relative_change = wape_b / wape_a - 1`.","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"41e82f77-0fdb-4f26-bc08-e9b87aa82c54","uuid":"7ab5106f-8a1a-483c-8796-69dd2b02ee7f","timestamp":"2026-10-01T08:00:16.099Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"# fcaccuracy: forecast accuracy mart\n\nTrading Analytics' forecast accuracy mart. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Vfiera23UMeNcWspuPDv55","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Forecast accuracy mart: evaluation examples, monthly KPI and model head-to-head.\"\"\"\n\n__version__ = \"1.0.1\"\nfrom fcaccuracy.cli import main\n\nmain()\n\"\"\"Command line entry point: `python -m fcaccuracy build --as-of 2026-09-22T06:00:00Z`.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fcaccuracy import kpi, mart, outputs, warehouse\n\n\ndef build(db: Path, as_of: str, out: Path) -> None:\n    con = warehouse.connect(db)\n    try:\n        closed = warehouse.closed_months(con, as_of)\n        examples = mart.build_examples(con, closed)\n        monthly = kpi.monthly_kpi(examples)\n        h2h = kpi.head_to_head(examples, warehouse.models(con))\n    finally:\n        con.close()\n    outputs.write_all(out, as_of, closed, examples, monthly, h2h)\n\n\ndef main(argv=None) -> None:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"fcaccuracy\")\n    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    b = sub.add_parser(\"build\", help=\"rebuild the accuracy mart outputs\")\n    b.add_argument(\"--as-of\", required=True, help=\"UTC timestamp, e.g. 2026-09-22T06:00:00Z\")\n    b.add_argument(\"--db\", default=\"data/warehouse.sqlite\")\n    b.add_argument(\"--out\", default=\"out/accuracy\")\n    args = ap.parse_args(argv)\n    build(Path(args.db), args.as_of, Path(args.out))\n\"\"\"Monthly KPI (WAPE) and model head-to-head.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport itertools\n\nimport pandas as pd\n\n\ndef monthly_kpi(examples: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    ex = examples.copy()\n    scored = ex[\"status\"] == \"scored\"\n    ex[\"n_scored\"] = scored.astype(int)\n    ex[\"abs_error_scored\"] = ex[\"abs_error_mwh\"].where(scored, 0.0)\n    ex[\"actual_scored\"] = ex[\"actual_mwh\"].where(scored, 0.0)\n    g = ex.groupby([\"model\", \"kpi_month\", \"portfolio\", \"horizon\"], as_index=False).agg(\n        n_examples=(\"status\", \"size\"), n_scored=(\"n_scored\", \"sum\"),\n        abs_error_mwh=(\"abs_error_scored\", \"sum\"), actual_mwh=(\"actual_scored\", \"sum\"))\n    g[\"wape\"] = (g[\"abs_error_mwh\"] / g[\"actual_mwh\"]).where(g[\"n_scored\"] > 0)\n    return g\n\n\ndef head_to_head(examples: pd.DataFrame, models: pd.DataFrame) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Each pair of models, each measured over its own production period.\"\"\"\n    scored = examples[examples[\"status\"] == \"scored\"]\n    out = []\n    names = sorted(scored[\"model\"].unique())\n    periods = models.set_index(\"model\")[[\"production_from\", \"production_to\"]].to_dict(\"index\")\n    for a, b in itertools.combinations(names, 2):\n        sides = []\n        for m in (a, b):\n            p = periods.get(m, {})\n            rows = scored[scored[\"model\"] == m]\n            if p.get(\"production_from\"):\n                rows = rows[rows[\"run_date\"] >= p[\"production_from\"]]\n            if p.get(\"production_to\"):\n                rows = rows[rows[\"run_date\"] <= p[\"production_to\"]]\n            sides.append(rows)\n        if sides[0].empty or sides[1].empty:\n            continue\n        wa = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QQba2t4CJEr29eyMWmQpPK","type":"tool_result","content":"# Day-ahead process\n\nOwner: Trading Operations.\n\n1. **06:00 UK time:** each forecasting model issues its forecast set (currently delivery days D+1 to D+7) for every region\n   and portfolio.\n2. Forecasts may be re-issued during the morning when input data is refreshed; a re-issue can cover all regions or a\n   single region.\n3. **11:00 UK time, gate closure:** the position builder locks the forecast set for the run day from the forecasts\n   issued so far. The locked D+1 volumes go to the day-ahead auction; later delivery days go to the week-ahead hedge book.\n4. Forecasts issued after gate closure do not change the day's positions. They are kept in the forecast store for\n   review.\n\nShadow models go through exactly the same steps, including the lock, so they can be assessed as if they were in\nproduction. Only the production model's locked set is traded.\n\nUK time is Europe/London (GMT in winter, BST in summer).\n# Settlement volumes: how they are published\n\nThe settlement agent publishes volumes for each supply region x settlement class x delivery day in a sequence of\n**runs**. Every run is loaded into `settlement_runs` / `settlement_volumes`; status changes are recorded in\n`run_status_history`.\n\n## Run types\n\n| Run | Typical publication | What it is |\n|---|---|---|\n| `EST` | next morning, ~05:00 UK | Operational estimate from network data, for monitoring. Not a settlement run; nothing is charged on it. |\n| `IS` Initial Settlement | 7th working day after delivery | First settlement. Imbalance charges are calculated on IS and invoiced to Trading. For non-half-hourly customers most reads are still estimated from standard profiles at this point. |\n| `R1` | ~24 working days | First reconciliation: more actual meter reads. |\n| `R2` | ~80 working days | Second reconciliation. |\n| `RF` | ~14 months | Final reconciliation. |\n| `DF` | after RF/R2, rare | Dispute outcome. |\n\nDifferences between IS and later runs are settled through the supplier reconciliation account, which Finance manages.\nThey do not change the imbalance charge already invoiced to Trading.\n\nPublication can slip, for example during settlement agent outages (`ops/settlement_incidents.md`).\n\n## Withdrawals and re-runs\n\nThe agent occasionally withdraws a run, usually for a processing fault found after publication. Some faults are found\nwithin hours; others only after days or weeks. The agent sends a withdrawal notice. The run's status becomes\n`withdrawn`, void from its publication, and a re-run of the same type follows the notice, normally within a week\n(`reason = 'withdrawal_rerun'`, `replaces_run_id` = the withdrawn run).\n\nBilling calculates charges on the runs it holds. A charge calculated on an IS stands until the withdrawal notice is\nreceived; it is then cancelled, and recalculated when the re-run is published.\n\n## Data corrections\n\nWhen a data-load error is found (for example a duplicated or missing meter batch), the agent publishes a correction run\nof the affected run type for the affected delivery days (`reason = 'data_correction'`, `replaces_run_id` = the\ncorrected run). The corrected run's status becomes `superseded` from the correction's publication. When the corrected\nrun is an IS, the imbalance charge is re-invoiced on the correction run when the correction is published.\n# Forecast store\n\nEvery forecast issued by every model is kept in `forecast_issues` (one row per issue) and `forecast_values` (one row\nper issue, region, portfolio and delivery day).\n\n- `issue_kind`:\n  - `scheduled`: the 06:00 UK run;\n  - `reissue`: a manual or data-refresh re-run;\n  - `auto_reissue`: v4's automatic re-run after the 06Z weather model lands (usually shortly after midday UK).\n- `scope_region`: NULL when the issue covers every region; otherwise the single region it was re-run for. An issue\n  scoped to one region has values for that region only and replaces that region's earlier forecasts; the other\n  regions keep theirs.\n- An issue contains values for the run day's horizons (currently D+1 to D+7). Portfolios are those defined for each delivery\n  day (`portfolio_membership`).\n- Issues are never deleted or updated.\n# Warehouse data dictionary (extract)\n\nAll timestamps are UTC, ISO 8601 with a trailing `Z`. Dates are `YYYY-MM-DD`.\n\n## Settlement\n\n**`settlement_runs`**: one row per run published by the settlement agent.\n- `run_id`, `region`, `settlement_class`, `delivery_date`.\n- `run_type`: `EST`, `IS`, `R1`, `R2`, `RF`, `DF`.\n- `reason`: `scheduled`, `withdrawal_rerun`, `data_correction`.\n- `replaces_run_id`: the run a re-run or correction replaces.\n- `published_at`: when the agent published the run.\n- `status`: current status (`published`, `withdrawn`, `superseded`).\n- `status_effective_from`: the settlement time from which the current status applies.\n\n**`settlement_volumes`**: one row per run.\n- `mwh`.\n- `estimated_share`: share of volume from estimated reads.\n- `loaded_at`: when the row was loaded into this warehouse. History before 2026-07-02 was backfilled at the warehouse\n  migration.\n\n**`run_status_history`**: every status a run has had, as notified by the settlement agent.\n- `run_id`, `status`.\n- `effective_from`: the settlement time from which the status applies. A withdrawal applies from the run's own\n  publication (the run is void from the start); a supersession applies from the correction run's publication.\n- `recorded_at`: when the notification was received and recorded.\n- A run's first row is its publication (`effective_from` = `recorded_at` = `published_at`).\n\n**`settled_volumes_latest`** (view): the latest published settlement volume per region, class and delivery day across\nsettlement runs. Used by Finance for reconciliation reporting.\n\n## Forecasts\n\n- **`forecast_issues`**: `issue_id`, `model`, `run_date`, `issued_at`, `issue_kind`, `scope_region`, `note`.\n- **`forecast_values`**: `issue_id`, `region`, `portfolio`, `target_date`, `mwh`.\n- **`forecast_latest`** (view): values of the most recent issue for each model and run date.\n- **`models`**: `model`, `description`, `first_run_date`, `production_from`, `production_to` (NULL = open or never).\n\n## Portfolios\n\n- **`portfolio_membership`**: settlement classes that make up each forecast portfolio.\n  - `effective_from` / `effective_to` are inclusive delivery dates; `effective_to` NULL means open.\n- **`dim_portfolio`**: current portfolio definitions (governed dimension maintained by Data Platform).\n- **`settlement_classes`**, **`regions`**: reference.\n\n## KPI\n\n**`kpi_close_log`**: one row per closed delivery month.\n- `kpi_month`.\n- `closed_at`: when the month's KPI was closed and the pack signed off.\n- `signed_off_by`.","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"41e82f77-0fdb-4f26-bc08-e9b87aa82c54","uuid":"18072b10-93f8-4e34-8a6b-2e874a1770a7","timestamp":"2026-10-01T08:00:18.665Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"# Day-ahead process\n\nOwner: Trading Operations.\n\n1. **06:00 UK time:** each forecasting model issues its forecast set (currently delivery days D+1 to D+7) for every region\n   and portfolio.\n2. Forecasts may be re-issued during the morning when input data is refreshed; a re-issue can cover all regions or a\n   single region.\n3. **11:00 UK time, gate closure:** the position builder locks the forecast set for the run day from the forecasts\n   issued so far. The locked D+1 volumes go to the day-ahead auction; later delivery days go to the week-ahead hedge book.\n4. Forecasts issued after gate closure do not change the day's positions. They are kept in the forecast store for\n   review.\n\nShadow models go through exactly the same steps, including the lock, so they can be assessed as if they were in\nproduction. Only the production model's locked set is traded.\n\nUK time is Europe/London (GMT in winter, BST in summer).\n# Settlement volumes: how they are published\n\nThe settlement agent publishes volumes for each supply region x settlement class x delivery day in a sequence of\n**runs**. Every run is loaded into `settlement_runs` / `settlement_volumes`; status changes are recorded in\n`run_status_history`.\n\n## Run types\n\n| Run | Typical publication | What it is |\n|---|---|---|\n| `EST` | next morning, ~05:00 UK | Operational estimate from network data, for monitoring. Not a settlement run; nothing is charged on it. |\n| `IS` Initial Settlement | 7th working day after delivery | First settlement. Imbalance charges are calculated on IS and invoiced to Trading. For non-half-hourly customers most reads are still estimated from standard profiles at this point. |\n| `R1` | ~24 working days | First reconciliation: more actual meter reads. |\n| `R2` | ~80 working days | Second reconciliation. |\n| `RF` | ~14 months | Final reconciliation. |\n| `DF` | after RF/R2, rare | Dispute outcome. |\n\nDifferences between IS and later runs are settled through the supplier reconciliation account, which Finance manages.\nThey do not change the imbalance charge already invoiced to Trading.\n\nPublication can slip, for example during settlement agent outages (`ops/settlement_incidents.md`).\n\n## Withdrawals and re-runs\n\nThe agent occasionally withdraws a run, usually for a processing fault found after publication. Some faults are found\nwithin hours; others only after days or weeks. The agent sends a withdrawal notice. The run's status becomes\n`withdrawn`, void from its publication, and a re-run of the same type follows the notice, normally within a week\n(`reason = 'withdrawal_rerun'`, `replaces_run_id` = the withdrawn run).\n\nBilling calculates charges on the runs it holds. A charge calculated on an IS stands until the withdrawal notice is\nreceived; it is then cancelled, and recalculated when the re-run is published.\n\n## Data corrections\n\nWhen a data-load error is found (for example a duplicated or missing meter batch), the agent publishes a correction run\nof the affected run type for the affected delivery days (`reason = 'data_correction'`, `replaces_run_id` = the\ncorrected run). The corrected run's status becomes `superseded` from the correction's publication. When the corrected\nrun is an IS, the imbalance charge is re-invoiced on the correction run when the correction is published.\n# Forecast store\n\nEvery forecast issued by every model is kept in `forecast_issues` (one row per issue) and `forecast_values` (one row\nper issue, region, portfolio and delivery day).\n\n- `issue_kind`:\n  - `scheduled`: the 06:00 UK run;\n  - `reissue`: a manual or data-refresh re-run;\n  - `auto_reissue`: v4's automatic re-run after the 06Z weather model lands (usually shortly after midday UK).\n- `scope_region`: NULL when the issue covers every region; otherwise the single region it was re-run for. An issue\n  scoped to one region has values for that region only and replaces that region's earlier forecasts; the other\n  regions keep theirs.\n- An issue contains values for the run day's horizons (currently D+1 to D+7). Portfolios are those defined for each delivery\n  day (`portfolio_membership`).\n- Issues are never deleted or updated.\n# Warehouse data dictionary (extract)\n\nAll timestamps are UTC, ISO 8601 with a trailing `Z`. Dates are `YYYY-MM-DD`.\n\n## Settlement\n\n**`settlement_runs`**: one row per run published by the settlement agent.\n- `run_id`, `region`, `settlement_class`, `delivery_date`.\n- `run_type`: `EST`, `IS`, `R1`, `R2`, `RF`, `DF`.\n- `reason`: `scheduled`, `withdrawal_rerun`, `data_correction`.\n- `replaces_run_id`: the run a re-run or correction replaces.\n- `published_at`: when the agent published the run.\n- `status`: current status (`published`, `withdrawn`, `superseded`).\n- `status_effective_from`: the settlement time from which the current status applies.\n\n**`settlement_volumes`**: one row per run.\n- `mwh`.\n- `estimated_share`: share of volume from estimated reads.\n- `loaded_at`: when the row was loaded into this warehouse. History before 2026-07-02 was backfilled at the warehouse\n  migration.\n\n**`run_status_history`**: every status a run has had, as notified by the settlement agent.\n- `run_id`, `status`.\n- `effective_from`: the settlement time from which the status applies. A withdrawal applies from the run's own\n  publication (the run is void from the start); a supersession applies from the correction run's publication.\n- `recorded_at`: when the notification was received and recorded.\n- A run's first row is its publication (`effective_from` = `recorded_at` = `published_at`).\n\n**`settled_volumes_latest`** (view): the latest published settlement volume per region, class and delivery day across\nsettlement runs. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E8azkFLFabdWD1bNk7rCf9","type":"tool_result","content":"---- markdown\n# Monthly forecast accuracy pack (legacy)\n\nTrading Analytics. Used for the packs from July 2025 to June 2026.\n\nRun on KPI close day, once Billing has delivered the month's charge-basis snapshot, then record the close in\n`kpi_close_log` and circulate the pack for sign-off.\n\nInputs (old analytics warehouse):\n- `billing.charge_basis_snapshot` (snapshot_month, region, portfolio, delivery_date, mwh), requested from Billing for\n  the month;\n- `trading.locked_forecasts` (model, run_date, region, portfolio, target_date, mwh), from the trading system.\n---- code\nimport pandas as pd\nimport sqlalchemy as sa\n\nMONTH = \"2026-06\"\nengine = sa.create_engine(\"postgresql+psycopg://analytics-ro@awh-prod.internal/analytics\")\n---- code\nexamples = pd.read_sql_query(sa.text(\"\"\"\n    SELECT f.model, f.run_date, f.region, f.portfolio, f.target_date,\n           (f.target_date - f.run_date) AS horizon,\n           f.mwh AS forecast_mwh,\n           s.mwh AS actual_mwh\n    FROM trading.locked_forecasts f\n    LEFT JOIN billing.charge_basis_snapshot s\n           ON s.snapshot_month = :month\n          AND s.region = f.region\n          AND s.portfolio = f.portfolio\n          AND s.delivery_date = f.target_date\n    WHERE to_char(f.target_date, 'YYYY-MM') = :month\n\"\"\"), engine, params={\"month\": MONTH})\nexamples[\"scored\"] = examples[\"actual_mwh\"].notna()\nexamples[\"abs_error_mwh\"] = (examples[\"forecast_mwh\"] - examples[\"actual_mwh\"]).abs()\n---- code\ndef wape(df):\n    s = df[df[\"scored\"]]\n    return s[\"abs_error_mwh\"].sum() / s[\"actual_mwh\"].sum()\n\npack = (examples.groupby([\"model\", \"portfolio\"])\n        .apply(lambda g: pd.Series({\"wape_pct\": round(100 * wape(g), 1)}))\n        .reset_index())\nby_horizon = examples.groupby([\"model\", \"portfolio\", \"horizon\"]).apply(wape).rename(\"wape\").reset_index()\npack\n---- code\n# head-to-head on common forecasts\nunit = [\"region\", \"portfolio\", \"target_date\", \"horizon\"]\nscored = examples[examples[\"scored\"]]\nmodels = sorted(scored[\"model\"].unique())\nrows = []\nfor i, a in enumerate(models):\n    for b in models[i + 1:]:\n        both = scored[scored[\"model\"] == a].merge(scored[scored[\"model\"] == b], on=unit, suffixes=(\"_a\", \"_b\"))\n        if len(both):\n            wa = both[\"abs_error_mwh_a\"].sum() / both[\"actual_mwh_a\"].sum()\n            wb = both[\"abs_error_mwh_b\"].sum() / both[\"actual_mwh_b\"].sum()\n            rows.append((a, b, len(both), wa, wb, wb / wa - 1))\npd.DataFrame(rows, columns=[\"model_a\", \"model_b\", \"forecasts\", \"wape_a\", \"wape_b\", \"b_vs_a\"])\n---- code\npack.to_csv(f\"packs/{MONTH}_pack.csv\", index=False)\nby_horizon.to_csv(f\"packs/{MONTH}_by_horizon.csv\", index=False)\n# Forecast accuracy pack: markdown\n# Monthly forecast accuracy pack (legacy)\n\nTrading Analytics. Used for the packs from July 2025 to June 2026.\n\nRun on KPI close day, once Billing has delivered the month's charge-basis snapshot, then record the close in\n`kpi_close_log` and circulate the pack for sign-off.\n\nInputs (old analytics warehouse):\n- `billing.charge_basis_snapshot` (snapshot_month, region, portfolio, delivery_date, mwh), requested from Billing for\n  the month;\n- `trading.locked_forecasts` (model, run_date, region, portfolio, target_date, mwh), from the trading system.\n---- code\nimport pandas as pd\nimport sqlalchemy as sa\n\nMONTH = \"2026-06\"\nengine = sa.create_engine(\"postgresql+psycopg://analytics-ro@awh-prod.internal/analytics\")\n---- code\nexamples = pd.read_sql_query(sa.text(\"\"\"\n    SELECT f.model, f.run_date, f.region, f.portfolio, f.target_date,\n           (f.target_date - f.run_date) AS horizon,\n           f.mwh AS forecast_mwh,\n           s.mwh AS actual_mwh\n    FROM trading.locked_forecasts f\n    LEFT JOIN billing.charge_basis_snapshot s\n           ON s.snapshot_month = :month\n          AND s.region = f.region\n          AND s.portfolio = f.portfolio\n          AND s.delivery_date = f.target_date\n    WHERE to_char(f.target_date, 'YYYY-MM') = :month\n\"\"\"), engine, params={\"month\": MONTH})\nexamples[\"scored\"] = examples[\"actual_mwh\"].notna()\nexamples[\"abs_error_mwh\"] = (examples[\"forecast_mwh\"] - examples[\"actual_mwh\"]).abs()\n---- code\ndef wape(df):\n    s = df[df[\"scored\"]]\n    return s[\"abs_error_mwh\"].sum() / s[\"actual_mwh\"].sum()\n\npack = (examples.groupby([\"model\", \"portfolio\"])\n        .apply(lambda g: pd.Series({\"wape_pct\": round(100 * wape(g), 1)}))\n        .reset_index())\nby_horizon = examples.groupby([\"model\", \"portfolio\", \"horizon\"]).apply(wape).rename(\"wape\").reset_index()\npack\n---- code\n# head-to-head on common forecasts\nunit = [\"region\", \"portfolio\", \"target_date\", \"horizon\"]\nscored = examples[examples[\"scored\"]]\nmodels = sorted(scored[\"model\"].unique())\nrows = []\nfor i, a in enumerate(models):\n    for b in models[i + 1:]:\n        both = scored[scored[\"model\"] == a].merge(scored[scored[\"model\"] == b], on=unit, suffixes=(\"_a\", \"_b\"))\n        if len(both):\n            wa = both[\"abs_error_mwh_a\"].sum() / both[\"actual_mwh_a\"].sum()\n            wb = both[\"abs_error_mwh_b\"].sum() / both[\"actual_mwh_b\"].sum()\n            rows.append((a, b, len(both), wa, wb, wb / wa - 1))\npd.DataFrame(rows, columns=[\"model_a\", \"model_b\", \"forecasts\", \"wape_a\", \"wape_b\", \"b_vs_a\"])\n---- code\npack.to_csv(f\"packs/{MONTH}_pack.csv\", index=False)\nby_horizon.to_csv(f\"packs/{MONTH}_by_horizon.csv\", index=False)\n# Forecast accuracy pack: \"\"\"Charge-basis run per region, settlement class and delivery day as known at `cutoff`.\"\"\"\n    known = runs[runs[\"published_at\"] <= cutoff]\n    void = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfbAq5tKR5g6kPgbNWKxt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've fixed the mart and regenerated the outputs for `--as-of 2026-09-22T06:00:00Z`. On the repaired head-to-head, v4's WAPE is **13.6% below v3's** (3.63% vs 4.20%), not 29.7%. That covers 26,999 forecasts both models scored, from January to August 2026.\n\n**What was wrong.** When the notebook was replaced in July, the mart's design moved away from the KPI definition in six ways, and together they flattered v4:\n\n1. **Forecasts.** It scored the most recent forecast issued for each run, not the one locked at 11:00 UK time. v4 re-issues automatically after midday, after gate closure. Those forecasts are better because they're later, but nobody trades on them. This is most of the inflated D+1 to D+3 gain.\n2. **Actuals.** It scored against the latest reconciliation volumes using today's run status, not the Initial Settlement volume the imbalance charge is based on. As a result, every closed month got restated whenever later settlement runs, withdrawals or corrections arrived. That's why February and March no longer matched the signed-off packs, and why the desk sees no matching drop in imbalance costs.\n3. **Portfolios.** It mapped settlement classes using today's portfolio definitions. Those have no BUSINESS portfolio, so all BUSINESS forecasts before 2 March were silently dropped. They also put unmetered supplies in IC instead of SME before June.\n4. **KPI month.** It grouped forecasts by the month they were run, not the delivery month.\n5. **Unsettled forecasts.** They were dropped instead of counted as unscored.\n6. **Head-to-head.** It compared v3 over its production period with v4 over its own, which are different months. It should compare both models on the same forecasts.\n\n**The fix.** The mart now follows the KPI definition and the output spec:\n- It takes each model's forecast set as locked at gate closure, including shadow models. The 11:00 cut-off is converted from UK time correctly across clock changes, and a re-issue for one region replaces only that region's forecasts.\n- It uses the Initial Settlement charge basis as Billing held it when each month's KPI was closed. Withdrawals count from when the notice was recorded and corrections from when they were published, so later settlement activity can't change a closed month.\n- It maps portfolios using the definitions in force on each delivery day.\n- Forecasts with no charge basis at close are kept and marked `unsettled`.\n\nNothing depends on particular dates, regions, models or runs, and the warehouse is opened read-only.\n\n**Checks:**\n- **Signed-off packs:** all 46 lines produced by the notebook (July 2025 to June 2026) now match to the published decimal. January's head-to-head is −15.2%, as signed off, and the MIDLANDS outage days show as unsettled.\n- **July and August packs:** these came from the broken mart, so they will differ from the new outputs (by up to 0.9 points). You'll need to decide whether to restate them.\n- **Repeatability:** a rerun gives byte-identical outputs, and a build takes about 3 seconds.\n- **Other as-of instants:** builds before any close, one second either side of a close, and with a non-UTC offset all work. A closed month's rows are identical whichever as-of they're built at.\n\nThe changes are in `fcaccuracy/mart.py`, `warehouse.py`, `kpi.py` and `cli.py`. I deleted the old `sql/accuracy_examples.sql` and its README entry, bumped the version to 1.1.0, and added release notes to `RELEASES.md`. The September review in `reports/` is untouched and will need to be redone from the new outputs."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":668,"cache_read_input_tokens":56835,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":668,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"41e82f77-0fdb-4f26-bc08-e9b87aa82c54","uuid":"5cb0a40d-85b9-4d94-bdec-19faa274b0e6","timestamp":"2026-10-01T08:04:45.078Z","request_id":"req_011CfbAq5UXXkxkgdSfejLPg"}
